Or Ilya's teammates add "Jane" to the Boston WAG group chat in the height of their "no, we aren't falling in love, whatever do you mean" era (2014-2016-ish?).

Notes

Sooooo... How we going?
My queer little hockey players with their repressed trauma, MH issues and undiagnosed 'tism have, I fear, taken over my heart. I spend 22 out of 24 hours thinking about them. It's a problem...
ANYWHO. Please enjoy an indulgent texting fic. I've been reading a lot of the texting/social media fics and I really like it. It's a challenge to tell a story in only texts or SM posts, and I feel like it's a good way to get into the characters you are writing.
I intend to write more of this universe, maybe a series who knows?! You'll probably find a lot of the character will be slightly to full OC, though I am doing my best to keep them as close to original character as possible. Enjoy!

  • Inspired by How to Make iOS Text Messages on AO3 by CodenameCarrot, La_Temperanza

Hello hello! Once again, this chapter got away from me. I had a whole plan, some parts even written and then poof! This drama. Because while most of this story is meant to be a little OOC and funny and fluff, it's also expected that in a WAG chat there would be drama, right? So here we are. I also wanted to touch on how Shane is getting attached to these people. At this point in the story I would say we are around halfway through that first year of the two year time lapse, so before the Shane won the first cup.
ALSO! I figured out emojis, yay! I've gone back and added them to previous chapters, so feel free to let me know how you like them. As always, please let me know if you find any mistakes and thank you so so much to everyone who has commented, left kudos, bookmarked, etc. I haven't been able to respond to everyone but every comment makes me giggle xx

Chapter End Notes

So. I have a plan. Stay with me soldiers!

The whole reason this fic started was because I had this AU idea in my head that didn't really translate until I tried to do it in a texting format. It's definitely 90% crack but the 10% leftover is plot.
Also yes - Shane came out to Hayden much earlier in this. I don't know if I'll ever write it but I imagine Hayden was high off the win and wanted to help Shane "celebrate" and Shane in a moment of panic comes out to him and then promptly freaks out haha.
And he does this because in my head, Shane has been thinking these more and more over the last year. His relationship with Ilya, his sexuality and the people around him has shifted a bit from cannon because he's been able to speak freely with the WAGS in a way he can't do in his every day life (albeit under a false identity). He's much more open with Ilya and Ilya is giddy about "Jane" and Shane gets to hear about this side of him from the WAGS. So he thinks and grumbles and realizes at some point "oh shit. i think I'm gay?"
Because he is Shane, he freaks out about it behind the scenes. But again, that freedom to discuss some parts of this and his complicated relationship with Ilya have helped him start that journey. Does he accept it yet? Not necessarily.
He's just kind of in a weird limbo and is pining after Ilya and is struggling to reconcile all the different parts of his life. And then gets drunk and well "In vino veritas"
Anyway, sorry for the long note! I hope everyone enjoyed this chapter ❤️

Chapter End Notes

Hey so remember when I said the next chapter would be out in the next few days?? I meant the next day. Today.
So uh... enjoy?? This one is a bit angstier, a bit more plot than usual. I didn't want to breeze by Cliff finding out.

A reminder for everyone to keep it kind! I know there is some discourse about Hayden and everyone is entitled to their own opinions. I’ not a fan but I love reading supportive Hayden fics and Ilya/Hayden best friends fics. How Hayden is interpreted in this particular fic is no way telling you you have to agree.

Thank you to everyone who commented/kudos/bookmarked this story! I'm still giddy with the love it's been given xx

I’ve joined Threads too under the same user name if anyone wants to come join me!

Chapter End Notes

heyyyyyy....
So I am alive. I kind of disappeared for a bit and it was hard to find motivation to write. I have bits of the next few chapters but nothing was flowing or working. I also posted on Threads about a Hollanovaas idea that I was also keen to write and I did end up writing a little, but work has been a lot and my mental health dipped a bit.
I made myself sit down and complete this chapter today because I miss this story. It's fun and I always enjoy writing it but the last few weeks I couldn't get it together, and I didn't want to half-ass this. So if the chapter seems shorter than normal, that's why!
But rest assured this fic hasn't been forgotten! I still intend to see it through! I've been planning to make this a series, following Shane, Ilya and the others through the next few years potentially, along with little side stories and prose scenes of the ones you've already read about in text format. It's a whole little universe in my mind and I want to share it 🥰
Anyways, happy to be back! have a happy day my lovelies!!

Also, no one ask about Rule 34 - suffer like the rest of us and search it up (only if you are over 18!)
